- **flexible-algebra**: A **flexible T-algebra** is a strict T-algebra that admits enough "room to move" — it is a retract (in the pseudo-morphism category T-Alg) of a free algebra. Flexible algebras are the key to constructing bicolimits in categories of algebras and establishing the BKP biadjunction theorem. ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/flexible-algebra) · ★64)
